### 7. payload_builder.py

```python
"""
PayloadBuilder - Payload Generation & Delivery
==============================================
Multi-platform payload generation and delivery system.
"""

import os
import re
import json
import time
import base64
import struct
import hashlib
import threading
import subprocess
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Tuple, Any, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto


class PayloadType(Enum):
    REVERSE_SHELL = auto()
    BIND_SHELL = auto()
    METERPRETER = auto()
    WEBSHELL = auto()
    STAGER = auto()
    DROPPER = auto()
    ROOTKIT = auto()
    KEYLOGGER = auto()
    RANSOMWARE = auto()
    TROJAN = auto()
    WORM = auto()
    BOT = auto()
    MINER = auto()
    SPYWARE = auto()
    ADWARE = auto()
    RAT = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class PayloadPlatform(Enum):
    WINDOWS = auto()
    LINUX = auto()
    MACOS = auto()
    ANDROID = auto()
    IOS = auto()
    BSD = auto()
    EMBEDDED = auto()
    WEB = auto()
    CROSS_PLATFORM = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class PayloadArch(Enum):
    X86 = auto()
    X64 = auto()
    ARM = auto()
    ARM64 = auto()
    MIPS = auto()
    MIPS64 = auto()
    PPC = auto()
    SPARC = auto()
    WASM = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class PayloadDelivery(Enum):
    DIRECT = auto()
    EMAIL = auto()
    WEB = auto()
    USB = auto()
    NETWORK = auto()
    SOCIAL = auto()
    SUPPLY_CHAIN = auto()
    WATERING_HOLE = auto()
    BROWSER = auto()
    APP_STORE = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


@dataclass
class Payload:
    """A generated payload."""
    id: str
    name: str
    payload_type: PayloadType
    platform: PayloadPlatform
    architecture: PayloadArch
    delivery: PayloadDelivery
    size: int = 0
    encoded: bool = False
    encrypted: bool = False
    obfuscated: bool = False
    shellcode: bytes = b""
    source_code: str = ""
    compiled: bool = False
    staged: bool = False
    stage_size: int = 0
    callbacks: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    features: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'name': self.name,
            'payload_type': self.payload_type.name,
            'platform': self.platform.name,
            'architecture': self.architecture.name,
            'delivery': self.delivery.name,
            'size': self.size,
            'encoded': self.encoded,
            'encrypted': self.encrypted,
            'obfuscated': self.obfuscated,
            'shellcode_b64': base64.b64encode(self.shellcode).decode() if self.shellcode else "",
            'compiled': self.compiled,
            'staged': self.staged,
            'stage_size': self.stage_size,
            'callbacks': self.callbacks,
            'features': self.features,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


@dataclass
class PayloadDelivery:
    """A payload delivery mechanism."""
    id: str
    payload_id: str
    method: PayloadDelivery
    target: str = ""
    status: str = "pending"
    delivered: bool = False
    executed: bool = False
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'payload_id': self.payload_id,
            'method': self.method.name,
            'target': self.target,
            'status': self.status,
            'delivered': self.delivered,
            'executed': self.executed,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


class PayloadBuilder:
    """Multi-platform payload builder."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.payloads: Dict[str, Payload] = {}
        self.deliveries: Dict[str, PayloadDelivery] = {}
        self.stats = {
            'payloads_generated': 0,
            'payloads_encoded': 0,
            'payloads_encrypted': 0,
            'payloads_delivered': 0,
            'payloads_executed': 0,
            'delivery_attempts': 0,
        }

    def generate(self, payload_type: PayloadType, platform: PayloadPlatform,
                 architecture: PayloadArch, delivery: PayloadDelivery,
                 callbacks: List[str] = None, features: List[str] = None) -> Payload:
        """Generate a new payload."""
        payload_id = f"pld_{int(time.time())}_{len(self.payloads)}"
        shellcode = b"\x90" * 512  # NOP sled placeholder
        payload = Payload(
            id=payload_id, name=f"{payload_type.name}_{platform.name}",
            payload_type=payload_type, platform=platform,
            architecture=architecture, delivery=delivery,
            size=len(shellcode), shellcode=shellcode,
            callbacks=callbacks or [], features=features or []
        )
        self.payloads[payload_id] = payload
        self.stats['payloads_generated'] += 1
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Generated {payload_type.name} for {platform.name}/{architecture.name}")
        return payload

    def encode(self, payload_id: str, encoder: str = "x64/xor") -> bool:
        """Encode a payload to evade detection."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return False
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Encoding {payload_id} with {encoder}")
        payload.encoded = True
        self.stats['payloads_encoded'] += 1
        return True

    def encrypt(self, payload_id: str, key: str = "") -> bool:
        """Encrypt a payload."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return False
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Encrypting {payload_id}")
        payload.encrypted = True
        self.stats['payloads_encrypted'] += 1
        return True

    def obfuscate(self, payload_id: str) -> bool:
        """Obfuscate a payload."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return False
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Obfuscating {payload_id}")
        payload.obfuscated = True
        return True

    def compile_payload(self, payload_id: str, compiler: str = "gcc") -> bool:
        """Compile source code payload."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return False
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Compiling {payload_id} with {compiler}")
        payload.compiled = True
        return True

    def deliver(self, payload_id: str, target: str, method: PayloadDelivery) -> PayloadDelivery:
        """Deliver a payload to a target."""
        delivery_id = f"dlv_{int(time.time())}"
        delivery = PayloadDelivery(
            id=delivery_id, payload_id=payload_id,
            method=method, target=target
        )
        self.deliveries[delivery_id] = delivery
        self.stats['delivery_attempts'] += 1
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Delivering {payload_id} to {target} via {method.name}")
        return delivery

    def mark_delivered(self, delivery_id: str) -> bool:
        """Mark a delivery as successful."""
        delivery = self.deliveries.get(delivery_id)
        if not delivery:
            return False
        delivery.delivered = True
        delivery.status = "delivered"
        self.stats['payloads_delivered'] += 1
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Delivery {delivery_id} confirmed")
        return True

    def mark_executed(self, delivery_id: str) -> bool:
        """Mark a payload as executed."""
        delivery = self.deliveries.get(delivery_id)
        if not delivery:
            return False
        delivery.executed = True
        delivery.status = "executed"
        self.stats['payloads_executed'] += 1
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Payload {delivery_id} executed")
        return True

    def generate_stager(self, platform: PayloadPlatform, callback_url: str,
                        stage_size: int = 4096) -> Payload:
        """Generate a stager payload."""
        payload = self.generate(
            PayloadType.STAGER, platform, PayloadArch.X64,
            PayloadDelivery.DIRECT, callbacks=[callback_url]
        )
        payload.staged = True
        payload.stage_size = stage_size
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Stager generated, stage size: {stage_size}")
        return payload

    def generate_reverse_shell(self, platform: PayloadPlatform, lhost: str,
                              lport: int, arch: PayloadArch = PayloadArch.X64) -> Payload:
        """Generate a reverse shell payload."""
        payload = self.generate(
            PayloadType.REVERSE_SHELL, platform, arch,
            PayloadDelivery.DIRECT, callbacks=[f"{lhost}:{lport}"],
            features=["TCP", "encrypted", "persistent"]
        )
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Reverse shell: {lhost}:{lport}")
        return payload

    def generate_webshell(self, language: str = "php", password: str = "jayjay") -> Payload:
        """Generate a web shell."""
        payload = self.generate(
            PayloadType.WEBSHELL, PayloadPlatform.WEB, PayloadArch.WASM,
            PayloadDelivery.WEB, features=[language, f"password={password}"]
        )
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] WebShell ({language}) generated")
        return payload

    def list_payloads(self) -> List[Payload]:
        """List all generated payloads."""
        return list(self.payloads.values())

    def list_deliveries(self) -> List[PayloadDelivery]:
        """List all deliveries."""
        return list(self.deliveries.values())

    def export_data(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        """Export payload data."""
        data = {
            'version': '21.0',
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'payloads': [p.to_dict() for p in self.payloads.values()],
            'deliveries': [d.to_dict() for d in self.deliveries.values()],
        }
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Export failed: {e}")
            return False

    def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        """Get builder statistics."""
        return dict(self.stats)
```

### 8. persistence.py

```python
"""
PersistenceManager - Persistence Mechanisms Across Platforms
=============================================================
Persistence mechanisms for all platforms and environments.
"""

import os
import re
import json
import time
import base64
import hashlib
import threading
import subprocess
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Tuple, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto


class PersistPlatform(Enum):
    WINDOWS = auto()
    LINUX = auto()
    MACOS = auto()
    ANDROID = auto()
    IOS = auto()
    WEB = auto()
    CLOUD = auto()
    CONTAINER = auto()
    EMBEDDED = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class PersistMethod(Enum):
    REGISTRY = auto()           # Windows Registry
    CRON = auto()             # Cron jobs
    SYSTEMD = auto()          # Systemd services
    LAUNCHD = auto()          # macOS launchd
    STARTUP_FOLDER = auto()   # Windows Startup
    WMI = auto()              # Windows Management Instrumentation
    SCHEDULED_TASK = auto()   # Windows Task Scheduler
    BASHRC = auto()           # .bashrc/.zshrc
    SSH_KEYS = auto()         # Authorized keys
    KERNEL_MODULE = auto()    # LKM rootkit
    LD_PRELOAD = auto()       # Library injection
    APP_INIT = auto()         # AppInit_DLLs
    DLL_HIJACK = auto()       # DLL hijacking
    SERVICE = auto()          # System service
    BROWSER_EXT = auto()      # Browser extension
    FIRMWARE = auto()         # Firmware modification
    BOOTKIT = auto()          # Boot sector
    UEFI = auto()             # UEFI implant
    CLOUD_FUNCTION = auto()   # Cloud function
    CONTAINER_IMAGE = auto()  # Poisoned image
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class PersistTarget(Enum):
    SYSTEM = auto()
    USER = auto()
    SERVICE = auto()
    KERNEL = auto()
    BOOT = auto()
    NETWORK = auto()
    BROWSER = auto()
    CLOUD = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


@dataclass
class PersistEntry:
    """A persistence entry."""
    id: str
    name: str
    method: PersistMethod
    platform: PersistPlatform
    target: PersistTarget
    path: str = ""
    command: str = ""
    enabled: bool = False
    detected: bool = False
    stealth_level: int = 5  # 1-10
    last_check: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'name': self.name,
            'method': self.method.name,
            'platform': self.platform.name,
            'target': self.target.name,
            'path': self.path,
            'command': self.command,
            'enabled': self.enabled,
            'detected': self.detected,
            'stealth_level': self.stealth_level,
            'last_check': self.last_check,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


class PersistenceManager:
    """Persistence mechanism manager."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.entries: Dict[str, PersistEntry] = {}
        self.stats = {
            'entries_created': 0,
            'entries_enabled': 0,
            'entries_detected': 0,
            'entries_removed': 0,
            'checks_performed': 0,
        }

    def create(self, name: str, method: PersistMethod, platform: PersistPlatform,
               target: PersistTarget, path: str, command: str,
               stealth_level: int = 5) -> PersistEntry:
        """Create a persistence entry."""
        entry_id = f"prs_{int(time.time())}_{len(self.entries)}"
        entry = PersistEntry(
            id=entry_id, name=name, method=method, platform=platform,
            target=target, path=path, command=command,
            stealth_level=stealth_level
        )
        self.entries[entry_id] = entry
        self.stats['entries_created'] += 1
        print(f"[PersistenceManager] Created {method.name} persistence: {name}")
        return entry

    def enable(self, entry_id: str) -> bool:
        """Enable a persistence entry."""
        entry = self.entries.get(entry_id)
        if not entry:
            return False
        entry.enabled = True
        self.stats['entries_enabled'] += 1
        print(f"[PersistenceManager] Enabled: {entry.name}")
        return True

    def disable(self, entry_id: str) -> bool:
        """Disable a persistence entry."""
        entry = self.entries.get(entry_id)
        if not entry:
            return False
        entry.enabled = False
        print(f"[PersistenceManager] Disabled: {entry.name}")
        return True

    def detect(self, entry_id: str) -> bool:
        """Detect if persistence is still active."""
        entry = self.entries.get(entry_id)
        if not entry:
            return False
        self.stats['checks_performed'] += 1
        entry.detected = entry.enabled
        if entry.detected:
            self.stats['entries_detected'] += 1
        return entry.detected

    def remove(self, entry_id: str) -> bool:
        """Remove a persistence entry."""
        entry = self.entries.pop(entry_id, None)
        if entry:
            self.stats['entries_removed'] += 1
            print(f"[PersistenceManager] Removed: {entry.name}")
            return True
        return False

    def create_systemd_service(self, name: str, command: str,
                               description: str = "System Service") -> PersistEntry:
        """Create a systemd service persistence."""
        path = f"/etc/systemd/system/{name}.service"
        service_content = f"""[Unit]
Description={description}
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=simple
ExecStart={command}
Restart=always
RestartSec=5

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target"""
        entry = self.create(
            name, PersistMethod.SYSTEMD, PersistPlatform.LINUX,
            PersistTarget.SYSTEM, path, command, stealth_level=7
        )
        entry.metadata['content'] = service_content
        return entry

    def create_cron_job(self, command: str, schedule: str = "* * * * *",
                        user: str = "root") -> PersistEntry:
        """Create a cron job persistence."""
        path = f"/var/spool/cron/crontabs/{user}"
        entry = self.create(
            "cron_persist", PersistMethod.CRON, PersistPlatform.LINUX,
            PersistTarget.USER, path, command, stealth_level=6
        )
        entry.metadata['schedule'] = schedule
        return entry

    def create_registry_run(self, name: str, command: str,
                            key: str = "HKCU\\Software\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\Run") -> PersistEntry:
        """Create Windows registry run key persistence."""
        entry = self.create(
            name, PersistMethod.REGISTRY, PersistPlatform.WINDOWS,
            PersistTarget.USER, key, command, stealth_level=5
        )
        return entry

    def create_ssh_backdoor(self, public_key: str, user: str = "root") -> PersistEntry:
        """Create SSH authorized_keys backdoor."""
        path = f"/{user}/.ssh/authorized_keys"
        entry = self.create(
            "ssh_backdoor", PersistMethod.SSH_KEYS, PersistPlatform.LINUX,
            PersistTarget.SYSTEM, path, public_key, stealth_level=8
        )
        return entry

    def create_ld_preload(self, library_path: str) -> PersistEntry:
        """Create LD_PRELOAD persistence."""
        entry = self.create(
            "ld_preload", PersistMethod.LD_PRELOAD, PersistPlatform.LINUX,
            PersistTarget.SYSTEM, "/etc/ld.so.preload", library_path,
            stealth_level=9
        )
        return entry

    def create_browser_extension(self, extension_id: str,
                                  browser: str = "chrome") -> PersistEntry:
        """Create browser extension persistence."""
        entry = self.create(
            "browser_ext", PersistMethod.BROWSER_EXT, PersistPlatform.WEB,
            PersistTarget.BROWSER, f"{browser}://extensions", extension_id,
            stealth_level=6
        )
        return entry

    def create_cloud_function(self, function_name: str, provider: str = "aws",
                               region: str = "us-east-1") -> PersistEntry:
        """Create cloud function persistence."""
        entry = self.create(
            function_name, PersistMethod.CLOUD_FUNCTION, PersistPlatform.CLOUD,
            PersistTarget.CLOUD, f"{provider}:{region}", function_name,
            stealth_level=7
        )
        return entry

    def check_all(self) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        """Check all persistence entries."""
        results = {}
        for entry_id, entry in self.entries.items():
            results[entry_id] = self.detect(entry_id)
        return results

    def list_all(self) -> List[PersistEntry]:
        """List all persistence entries."""
        return list(self.entries.values())

    def export_data(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        """Export persistence data."""
        data = {
            'version': '21.0',
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'entries': [e.to_dict() for e in self.entries.values()],
        }
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[PersistenceManager] Export failed: {e}")
            return False

    def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        """Get manager statistics."""
        return dict(self.stats)
```

### 9. reconnaissance.py

```python
"""
ReconEngine - Global Reconnaissance Engine
===========================================
Comprehensive reconnaissance for all targets and platforms.
"""

import os
import re
import json
import time
import socket
import struct
import hashlib
import threading
import subprocess
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Tuple, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto


class ReconMethod(Enum):
    PASSIVE = auto()        # OSINT, passive collection
    ACTIVE = auto()         # Direct scanning, probing
    SOCIAL = auto()         # Social engineering
    PHYSICAL = auto()       # Physical reconnaissance
    TECHNICAL = auto()      # Technical analysis
    HUMINT = auto()         # Human intelligence
    SIGINT = auto()         # Signals intelligence
    IMINT = auto()          # Imagery intelligence
    CYBER = auto()          # Cyber reconnaissance
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class ReconTarget(Enum):
    INDIVIDUAL = auto()
    ORGANIZATION = auto()
    INFRASTRUCTURE = auto()
    NETWORK = auto()
    APPLICATION = auto()
    DEVICE = auto()
    GEOGRAPHIC = auto()
    SATELLITE = auto()
    IOT = auto()
    MOBILE = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


@dataclass
class ReconTarget:
    """A reconnaissance target."""
    id: str
    name: str
    target_type: ReconTarget
    methods: List[ReconMethod] = field(default_factory=list)
    ip_ranges: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    domains: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    emails: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    employees: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    technologies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    vulnerabilities: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    findings: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    last_scan: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'name': self.name,
            'target_type': self.target_type.name,
            'methods': [m.name for m in self.methods],
            'ip_ranges': self.ip_ranges,
            'domains': self.domains,
            'emails': self.emails,
            'employees': self.employees,
            'technologies': self.technologies,
            'vulnerabilities': self.vulnerabilities,
            'findings': self.findings,
            'last_scan': self.last_scan,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


class ReconEngine:
    """Global reconnaissance engine."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.targets: Dict[str, ReconTarget] = {}
        self.stats = {
            'targets_added': 0,
            'scans_performed': 0,
            'domains_discovered': 0,
            'emails_discovered': 0,
            'employees_discovered': 0,
            'technologies_found': 0,
            'vulnerabilities_found': 0,
            'findings_recorded': 0,
        }

    def add_target(self, target_id: str, name: str,
                   target_type: ReconTarget) -> ReconTarget:
        """Add a reconnaissance target."""
        target = ReconTarget(
            id=target_id, name=name, target_type=target_type
        )
        self.targets[target_id] = target
        self.stats['targets_added'] += 1
        print(f"[ReconEngine] Target added: {name}")
        return target

    def passive_recon(self, target_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Perform passive reconnaissance."""
        target = self.targets.get(target_id)
        if not target:
            return {}
        target.methods.append(ReconMethod.PASSIVE)
        self.stats['scans_performed'] += 1
        print(f"[ReconEngine] Passive recon on {target.name}")
        findings = {
            'domains': [f"{target.name.lower().replace(' ', '')}.com",
                       f"{target.name.lower().replace(' ', '')}.net"],
            'subdomains': ["www", "mail", "ftp", "api", "dev", "staging"],
            'whois': {'registrar': 'GoDaddy', 'created': '2010-01-01'},
            'dns_records': {'A': ['1.2.3.4'], 'MX': ['mail.example.com']},
            'social_media': ['linkedin', 'twitter', 'facebook'],
        }
        target.domains.extend(findings['domains'])
        self.stats['domains_discovered'] += len(findings['domains'])
        target.findings.append({'type': 'passive', 'data': findings})
        return findings

    def active_recon(self, target_id: str, ip_range: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Perform active reconnaissance."""
        target = self.targets.get(target_id)
        if not target:
            return {}
        target.methods.append(ReconMethod.ACTIVE)
        target.ip_ranges.append(ip_range)
        self.stats['scans_performed'] += 1
        print(f"[ReconEngine] Active recon on {ip_range}")
        findings = {
            'live_hosts': ['192.168.1.1', '192.168.1.10', '192.168.1.100'],
            'open_ports': {80: 'HTTP', 443: 'HTTPS', 22: 'SSH', 3389: 'RDP'},
            'services': ['Apache 2.4.41', 'OpenSSH 8.2p1', 'Microsoft IIS 10.0'],
            'os_fingerprints': ['Linux 5.4', 'Windows 10', 'Windows Server 2019'],
        }
        target.findings.append({'type': 'active', 'data': findings})
        return findings

    def social_recon(self, target_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Perform social reconnaissance."""
        target = self.targets.get(target_id)
        if not target:
            return {}
        target.methods.append(ReconMethod.SOCIAL)
        self.stats['scans_performed'] += 1
        print(f"[ReconEngine] Social recon on {target.name}")
        employees = [
            {'name': 'John Doe', 'title': 'CEO', 'email': 'john@example.com',
             'linkedin': 'linkedin.com/in/johndoe'},
            {'name': 'Jane Smith', 'title': 'CTO', 'email': 'jane@example.com',
             'linkedin': 'linkedin.com/in/janesmith'},
            {'name': 'Bob Wilson', 'title': 'IT Admin', 'email': 'bob@example.com',
             'linkedin': 'linkedin.com/in/bobwilson'},
        ]
        target.employees.extend(employees)
        target.emails.extend([e['email'] for e in employees])
        self.stats['employees_discovered'] += len(employees)
        self.stats['emails_discovered'] += len(employees)
        target.findings.append({'type': 'social', 'data': employees})
        return {'employees': employees}

    def tech_recon(self, target_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Perform technology reconnaissance."""
        target = self.targets.get(target_id)
        if not target:
            return {}
        target.methods.append(ReconMethod.TECHNICAL)
        self.stats['scans_performed'] += 1
        print(f"[ReconEngine] Tech recon on {target.name}")
        technologies = [
            'Apache 2.4.41', 'PHP 7.4.3', 'MySQL 8.0.19',
            'jQuery 3.5.1', 'Bootstrap 4.5.0', 'WordPress 5.4',
            'Nginx 1.18.0', 'Node.js 14.15.0', 'React 17.0.1',
        ]
        target.technologies.extend(technologies)
        self.stats['technologies_found'] += len(technologies)
        target.findings.append({'type': 'technical', 'data': technologies})
        return {'technologies': technologies}

    def find_vulnerabilities(self, target_id: str) -> List[str]:
        """Find vulnerabilities for target technologies."""
        target = self.targets.get(target_id)
        if not target:
            return []
        vulns = [
            'CVE-2021-41773 - Apache Path Traversal',
            'CVE-2021-44228 - Log4j RCE',
            'CVE-2020-1472 - Zerologon',
            'CVE-2019-19781 - Citrix ADC RCE',
            'CVE-2020-0688 - Exchange RCE',
        ]
        target.vulnerabilities.extend(vulns)
        self.stats['vulnerabilities_found'] += len(vulns)
        return vulns

    def full_recon(self, target_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Perform full reconnaissance."""
        return {
            'passive': self.passive_recon(target_id),
            'active': self.active_recon(target_id, "192.168.1.0/24"),
            'social': self.social_recon(target_id),
            'technical': self.tech_recon(target_id),
            'vulnerabilities': self.find_vulnerabilities(target_id),
        }

    def list_targets(self) -> List[ReconTarget]:
        """List all targets."""
        return list(self.targets.values())

    def get_target(self, target_id: str) -> Optional[ReconTarget]:
        """Get a specific target."""
        return self.targets.get(target_id)

    def export_data(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        """Export reconnaissance data."""
        data = {
            'version': '21.0',
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'targets': [t.to_dict() for t in self.targets.values()],
        }
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ReconEngine] Export failed: {e}")
            return False

    def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        """Get engine statistics."""
        return dict(self.stats)
```

### 10. crypto_ops.py

```python
"""
CryptoEngine - Cryptographic Operations & Key Extraction
=========================================================
Advanced cryptographic operations, key extraction, and cryptanalysis.
"""

import os
import re
import json
import time
import base64
import hashlib
import hmac
import struct
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Tuple, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto


class CryptoMethod(Enum):
    AES = auto()
    RSA = auto()
    ECC = auto()
    CHACHA20 = auto()
    BLOWFISH = auto()
    TWOFISH = auto()
    SERPENT = auto()
    DES = auto()
    TDES = auto()
    RC4 = auto()
    CAMELLIA = auto()
    IDEA = auto()
    DH = auto()
    ECDH = auto()
    DSA = auto()
    ECDSA = auto()
    ED25519 = auto()
    X25519 = auto()
    BLAKE2 = auto()
    SHA256 = auto()
    SHA512 = auto()
    SHA3 = auto()
    MD5 = auto()
    RIPEMD = auto()
    WHIRLPOOL = auto()
    PBKDF2 = auto()
    SCRYPT = auto()
    ARGON2 = auto()
    BCRYPT = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class CryptoTarget(Enum):
    FILE = auto()
    DISK = auto()
    MEMORY = auto()
    NETWORK = auto()
    KEYSTORE = auto()
    CERTIFICATE = auto()
    PASSWORD = auto()
    TOKEN = auto()
    WALLET = auto()
    DATABASE = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


@dataclass
class CryptoTarget:
    """A cryptographic target."""
    id: str
    name: str
    target_type: CryptoTarget
    encryption: CryptoMethod = CryptoMethod.UNKNOWN
    key_size: int = 0
    hash_type: CryptoMethod = CryptoMethod.UNKNOWN
    salt: bytes = b""
    iterations: int = 0
    extracted: bool = False
    cracked: bool = False
    plaintext: str = ""
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'name': self.name,
            'target_type': self.target_type.name,
            'encryption': self.encryption.name,
            'key_size': self.key_size,
            'hash_type': self.hash_type.name,
            'salt_b64': base64.b64encode(self.salt).decode() if self.salt else "",
            'iterations': self.iterations,
            'extracted': self.extracted,
            'cracked': self.cracked,
            'plaintext': self.plaintext,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


class CryptoEngine:
    """Cryptographic operations engine."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.targets: Dict[str, CryptoTarget] = {}
        self.keys: Dict[str, bytes] = {}
        self.stats = {
            'targets_added': 0,
            'keys_extracted': 0,
            'passwords_cracked': 0,
            'hashes_computed': 0,
            'encrypted_files': 0,
            'decrypted_files': 0,
        }

    def add_target(self, target_id: str, name: str,
                   target_type: CryptoTarget) -> CryptoTarget:
        """Add a cryptographic target."""
        target = CryptoTarget(
            id=target_id, name=name, target_type=target_type
        )
        self.targets[target_id] = target
        self.stats['targets_added'] += 1
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Target added: {name}")
        return target

    def extract_key(self, target_id: str, key_data: bytes) -> bool:
        """Extract a key from a target."""
        target = self.targets.get(target_id)
        if not target:
            return False
        target.extracted = True
        self.keys[target_id] = key_data
        self.stats['keys_extracted'] += 1
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Key extracted from {target.name}")
        return True

    def crack_password(self, target_id: str, hash_value: str,
                       wordlist: str = "/usr/share/wordlists/rockyou.txt") -> Optional[str]:
        """Crack a password hash."""
        target = self.targets.get(target_id)
        if not target:
            return None
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Cracking {target.name}...")
        self.stats['passwords_cracked'] += 1
        target.cracked = True
        target.plaintext = "password123"
        return target.plaintext

    def brute_force(self, target_id: str, charset: str = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz",
                    min_len: int = 1, max_len: int = 8) -> Optional[str]:
        """Brute force a password."""
        target = self.targets.get(target_id)
        if not target:
            return None
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Brute forcing {target.name}...")
        self.stats['passwords_cracked'] += 1
        target.cracked = True
        target.plaintext = "abc123"
        return target.plaintext

    def rainbow_table(self, target_id: str, table_path: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Use rainbow tables to crack a hash."""
        target = self.targets.get(target_id)
        if not target:
            return None
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Rainbow table attack on {target.name}...")
        self.stats['passwords_cracked'] += 1
        target.cracked = True
        target.plaintext = "letmein"
        return target.plaintext

    def hash_file(self, filepath: str, method: CryptoMethod = CryptoMethod.SHA256) -> str:
        """Hash a file."""
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Hashing {filepath} with {method.name}")
        self.stats['hashes_computed'] += 1
        return hashlib.sha256(b"file_content").hexdigest()

    def encrypt_file(self, filepath: str, key: bytes,
                     method: CryptoMethod = CryptoMethod.AES) -> bool:
        """Encrypt a file."""
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Encrypting {filepath} with {method.name}")
        self.stats['encrypted_files'] += 1
        return True

    def decrypt_file(self, filepath: str, key: bytes,
                     method: CryptoMethod = CryptoMethod.AES) -> bool:
        """Decrypt a file."""
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Decrypting {filepath} with {method.name}")
        self.stats['decrypted_files'] += 1
        return True

    def generate_key(self, method: CryptoMethod, size: int = 256) -> bytes:
        """Generate a cryptographic key."""
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Generating {size}-bit {method.name} key")
        return os.urandom(size // 8)

    def derive_key(self, password: str, salt: bytes = None,
                   iterations: int = 100000) -> bytes:
        """Derive a key from password using PBKDF2."""
        if salt is None:
            salt = os.urandom(32)
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Deriving key with PBKDF2 ({iterations} iterations)")
        return hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac('sha256', password.encode(), salt, iterations)

    def hmac_sign(self, data: bytes, key: bytes) -> bytes:
        """Create HMAC signature."""
        return hmac.new(key, data, hashlib.sha256).digest()

    def hmac_verify(self, data: bytes, key: bytes, signature: bytes) -> bool:
        """Verify HMAC signature."""
        expected = hmac.new(key, data, hashlib.sha256).digest()
        return hmac.compare_digest(expected, signature)

    def extract_from_memory(self, pid: int, pattern: bytes) -> List[bytes]:
        """Extract keys from process memory."""
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Extracting from PID {pid}")
        return [b"extracted_key_1", b"extracted_key_2"]

    def extract_from_keystore(self, keystore_path: str,
                              password: str = "") -> Dict[str, bytes]:
        """Extract keys from a keystore."""
        print(f"[CryptoEngine] Extracting from keystore: {keystore_path}")
        return {'private_key': b'key_data', 'certificate': b'cert_data'}

    def list_targets(self) -> List[CryptoTarget]:
        """List all targets."""
        return list(self.targets.values())

    def export_data(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        """Export crypto data."""
        data = {
            'version': '21.0',
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'targets': [t.to_dict() for t in self.targets.values()],
        }
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[CryptoEngine] Export failed: {e}")
            return False

    def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        """Get engine statistics."""
        return dict(self.stats)
```

### 11. steganography.py

```python
"""
StegoEngine - Steganographic Communication
===========================================
Hide and extract data in images, audio, video, and other carriers.
"""

import os
import re
import json
import time
import base64
import struct
import hashlib
import threading
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Tuple, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto


class StegoMethod(Enum):
    LSB = auto()            # Least Significant Bit
    DCT = auto()            # Discrete Cosine Transform
    DWT = auto()            # Discrete Wavelet Transform
    ECHO = auto()           # Echo hiding (audio)
    PHASE = auto()          # Phase coding (audio)
    SPREAD_SPECTRUM = auto()
    PARITY = auto()
    QUANTIZATION = auto()
    PATCHWORK = auto()
    TXT_WHITESPACE = auto() # Whitespace steganography
    TXT_FORMATTING = auto() # Formatting-based
    TXT_UNICODE = auto()    # Unicode homoglyphs
    TXT_ZERO_WIDTH = auto() # Zero-width characters
    NETWORK_TIMING = auto() # Covert timing channels
    NETWORK_STORAGE = auto() # Covert storage channels
    DNS_TUNNEL = auto()
    ICMP_TUNNEL = auto()
    HTTP_TUNNEL = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class StegoCarrier(Enum):
    IMAGE = auto()
    AUDIO = auto()
    VIDEO = auto()
    TEXT = auto()
    NETWORK = auto()
    FILE_SYSTEM = auto()
    DNA = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


@dataclass
class StegoPayload:
    """A steganographic payload."""
    id: str
    data: bytes
    carrier_type: StegoCarrier
    method: StegoMethod
    carrier_file: str = ""
    capacity: int = 0
    psnr: float = 0.0
    embedded: bool = False
    extracted: bool = False
    password: str = ""
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'data_b64': base64.b64encode(self.data).decode() if self.data else "",
            'carrier_type': self.carrier_type.name,
            'method': self.method.name,
            'carrier_file': self.carrier_file,
            'capacity': self.capacity,
            'psnr': self.psnr,
            'embedded': self.embedded,
            'extracted': self.extracted,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


class StegoEngine:
    """Steganographic communication engine."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.payloads: Dict[str, StegoPayload] = {}
        self.stats = {
            'payloads_created': 0,
            'payloads_embedded': 0,
            'payloads_extracted': 0,
            'carriers_processed': 0,
            'bytes_hidden': 0,
            'bytes_extracted': 0,
        }

    def create_payload(self, data: bytes, carrier_type: StegoCarrier,
                      method: StegoMethod) -> StegoPayload:
        """Create a steganographic payload."""
        payload_id = f"stg_{int(time.time())}_{len(self.payloads)}"
        payload = StegoPayload(
            id=payload_id, data=data, carrier_type=carrier_type,
            method=method, capacity=len(data) * 8
        )
        self.payloads[payload_id] = payload
        self.stats['payloads_created'] += 1
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Payload created: {len(data)} bytes, {carrier_type.name}/{method.name}")
        return payload

    def embed_lsb_image(self, payload_id: str, image_path: str,
                        output_path: str) -> bool:
        """Embed payload using LSB in image."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return False
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Embedding {payload_id} into {image_path} (LSB)")
        payload.carrier_file = output_path
        payload.embedded = True
        self.stats['payloads_embedded'] += 1
        self.stats['bytes_hidden'] += len(payload.data)
        return True

    def embed_dct_image(self, payload_id: str, image_path: str,
                        output_path: str) -> bool:
        """Embed payload using DCT in image."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return False
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Embedding {payload_id} into {image_path} (DCT)")
        payload.carrier_file = output_path
        payload.embedded = True
        self.stats['payloads_embedded'] += 1
        self.stats['bytes_hidden'] += len(payload.data)
        return True

    def embed_audio_echo(self, payload_id: str, audio_path: str,
                         output_path: str) -> bool:
        """Embed payload using echo hiding in audio."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return False
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Embedding {payload_id} into {audio_path} (Echo)")
        payload.carrier_file = output_path
        payload.embedded = True
        self.stats['payloads_embedded'] += 1
        self.stats['bytes_hidden'] += len(payload.data)
        return True

    def embed_text_whitespace(self, payload_id: str, text: str) -> str:
        """Embed payload using whitespace in text."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return text
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Embedding {payload_id} into text (whitespace)")
        # Add zero-width spaces and tabs
        stego_text = text.replace(" ", " \t").replace("\n", " \n")
        payload.embedded = True
        self.stats['payloads_embedded'] += 1
        return stego_text

    def embed_text_unicode(self, payload_id: str, text: str) -> str:
        """Embed payload using Unicode homoglyphs."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return text
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Embedding {payload_id} into text (Unicode)")
        # Replace some characters with homoglyphs
        homoglyphs = {'a': 'а', 'e': 'е', 'o': 'о', 'p': 'р', 'c': 'с'}
        stego_text = "".join(homoglyphs.get(c, c) for c in text)
        payload.embedded = True
        self.stats['payloads_embedded'] += 1
        return stego_text

    def extract_lsb_image(self, payload_id: str, image_path: str) -> Optional[bytes]:
        """Extract payload from LSB image."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return None
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Extracting {payload_id} from {image_path} (LSB)")
        payload.extracted = True
        self.stats['payloads_extracted'] += 1
        self.stats['bytes_extracted'] += len(payload.data)
        return payload.data

    def extract_audio_echo(self, payload_id: str, audio_path: str) -> Optional[bytes]:
        """Extract payload from echo-hidden audio."""
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload:
            return None
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Extracting {payload_id} from {audio_path} (Echo)")
        payload.extracted = True
        self.stats['payloads_extracted'] += 1
        return payload.data

    def create_dns_tunnel(self, domain: str, data: bytes) -> List[str]:
        """Create DNS tunnel queries."""
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Creating DNS tunnel to {domain}")
        encoded = base64.b32encode(data).decode()
        chunks = [encoded[i:i+63] for i in range(0, len(encoded), 63)]
        queries = [f"{chunk}.{domain}" for chunk in chunks]
        self.stats['bytes_hidden'] += len(data)
        return queries

    def create_icmp_tunnel(self, data: bytes, target_ip: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """Create ICMP tunnel packets."""
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Creating ICMP tunnel to {target_ip}")
        chunks = [data[i:i+16] for i in range(0, len(data), 16)]
        packets = []
        for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
            packets.append({
                'type': 8, 'code': 0, 'id': i,
                'data': base64.b64encode(chunk).decode()
            })
        self.stats['bytes_hidden'] += len(data)
        return packets

    def create_http_tunnel(self, data: bytes, url: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """Create HTTP tunnel requests."""
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Creating HTTP tunnel to {url}")
        encoded = base64.b64encode(data).decode()
        requests = []
        for i in range(0, len(encoded), 1000):
            chunk = encoded[i:i+1000]
            requests.append({
                'method': 'POST',
                'url': url,
                'headers': {'X-Data': chunk},
                'body': f"data={chunk[:100]}"
            })
        self.stats['bytes_hidden'] += len(data)
        return requests

    def analyze_capacity(self, carrier_path: str,
                         carrier_type: StegoCarrier) -> int:
        """Analyze steganographic capacity of a carrier."""
        print(f"[StegoEngine] Analyzing capacity of {carrier_path}")
        self.stats['carriers_processed'] += 1
        return 1024 * 1024  # 1MB placeholder

    def list_payloads(self) -> List[StegoPayload]:
        """List all payloads."""
        return list(self.payloads.values())

    def export_data(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        """Export steganography data."""
        data = {
            'version': '21.0',
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'payloads': [p.to_dict() for p in self.payloads.values()],
        }
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[StegoEngine] Export failed: {e}")
            return False

    def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        """Get engine statistics."""
        return dict(self.stats)
```

### 12. c2_server.py

```python
"""
C2Server - Command & Control Server
====================================
Command and control infrastructure for botnet operations.
"""

import os
import re
import json
import time
import base64
import hashlib
import socket
import struct
import threading
import subprocess
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Tuple, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto


class C2Protocol(Enum):
    HTTP = auto()
    HTTPS = auto()
    DNS = auto()
    TCP = auto()
    UDP = auto()
    ICMP = auto()
    WEBSOCKET = auto()
    GRPC = auto()
    TOR = auto()
    I2P = auto()
    MQTT = auto()
    COAP = auto()
    BLUETOOTH = auto()
    RADIO = auto()
    SATELLITE = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class C2ChannelType(Enum):
    DIRECT = auto()
    PROXY = auto()
    CDN = auto()
    CLOUD = auto()
    P2P = auto()
    MESH = auto()
    DEAD_DROP = auto()
    STEGANOGRAPHY = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


@dataclass
class C2Client:
    """A C2 client/bot."""
    id: str
    hostname: str = ""
    ip: str = ""
    os: str = ""
    architecture: str = ""
    username: str = ""
    privilege: str = "user"
    last_seen: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    status: str = "online"
    capabilities: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    tasks: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'hostname': self.hostname,
            'ip': self.ip,
            'os': self.os,
            'architecture': self.architecture,
            'username': self.username,
            'privilege': self.privilege,
            'last_seen': self.last_seen,
            'status': self.status,
            'capabilities': self.capabilities,
            'tasks': self.tasks,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


@dataclass
class C2Channel:
    """A C2 communication channel."""
    id: str
    name: str
    protocol: C2Protocol
    channel_type: C2ChannelType
    endpoint: str = ""
    active: bool = False
    encrypted: bool = True
    heartbeat_interval: int = 60
    clients: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'name': self.name,
            'protocol': self.protocol.name,
            'channel_type': self.channel_type.name,
            'endpoint': self.endpoint,
            'active': self.active,
            'encrypted': self.encrypted,
            'heartbeat_interval': self.heartbeat_interval,
            'clients': self.clients,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


class C2Server:
    """Command and Control server."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.clients: Dict[str, C2Client] = {}
        self.channels: Dict[str, C2Channel] = {}
        self.tasks: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
        self.stats = {
            'clients_registered': 0,
            'clients_online': 0,
            'channels_active': 0,
            'tasks_issued': 0,
            'tasks_completed': 0,
            'data_received': 0,
            'data_sent': 0,
        }

    def create_channel(self, channel_id: str, name: str,
                       protocol: C2Protocol, channel_type: C2ChannelType,
                       endpoint: str) -> C2Channel:
        """Create a C2 channel."""
        channel = C2Channel(
            id=channel_id, name=name, protocol=protocol,
            channel_type=channel_type, endpoint=endpoint
        )
        self.channels[channel_id] = channel
        print(f"[C2Server] Channel created: {name} ({protocol.name})")
        return channel

    def start_channel(self, channel_id: str) -> bool:
        """Start a C2 channel."""
        channel = self.channels.get(channel_id)
        if not channel:
            return False
        channel.active = True
        self.stats['channels_active'] += 1
        print(f"[C2Server] Channel started: {channel.name}")
        return True

    def stop_channel(self, channel_id: str) -> bool:
        """Stop a C2 channel."""
        channel = self.channels.get(channel_id)
        if not channel:
            return False
        channel.active = False
        self.stats['channels_active'] -= 1
        print(f"[C2Server] Channel stopped: {channel.name}")
        return True

    def register_client(self, client_id: str, hostname: str, ip: str,
                        os: str, architecture: str, username: str = "",
                        capabilities: List[str] = None) -> C2Client:
        """Register a new client."""
        client = C2Client(
            id=client_id, hostname=hostname, ip=ip, os=os,
            architecture=architecture, username=username,
            capabilities=capabilities or []
        )
        self.clients[client_id] = client
        self.stats['clients_registered'] += 1
        self.stats['clients_online'] += 1
        print(f"[C2Server] Client registered: {hostname} ({ip})")
        return client

    def client_heartbeat(self, client_id: str) -> bool:
        """Process client heartbeat."""
        client = self.clients.get(client_id)
        if not client:
            return False
        client.last_seen = time.time()
        client.status = "online"
        return True

    def client_offline(self, client_id: str) -> bool:
        """Mark client as offline."""
        client = self.clients.get(client_id)
        if not client:
            return False
        client.status = "offline"
        self.stats['clients_online'] -= 1
        return True

    def issue_task(self, client_id: str, task_type: str,
                   parameters: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """Issue a task to a client."""
        task_id = f"task_{int(time.time())}_{len(self.tasks)}"
        task = {
            'id': task_id,
            'client_id': client_id,
            'type': task_type,
            'parameters': parameters,
            'status': 'pending',
            'created': time.time(),
            'completed': None,
            'result': None,
        }
        self.tasks[task_id] = task
        client = self.clients.get(client_id)
        if client:
            client.tasks.append(task)
        self.stats['tasks_issued'] += 1
        print(f"[C2Server] Task issued to {client_id}: {task_type}")
        return task_id

    def task_complete(self, task_id: str, result: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """Mark a task as completed."""
        task = self.tasks.get(task_id)
        if not task:
            return False
        task['status'] = 'completed'
        task['completed'] = time.time()
        task['result'] = result
        self.stats['tasks_completed'] += 1
        print(f"[C2Server] Task completed: {task_id}")
        return True

    def broadcast_task(self, task_type: str, parameters: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:
        """Broadcast a task to all online clients."""
        task_ids = []
        for client_id, client in self.clients.items():
            if client.status == "online":
                task_id = self.issue_task(client_id, task_type, parameters)
                task_ids.append(task_id)
        print(f"[C2Server] Broadcast to {len(task_ids)} clients")
        return task_ids

    def get_client_output(self, client_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """Get output from a client."""
        client = self.clients.get(client_id)
        if not client:
            return []
        return [t for t in client.tasks if t['status'] == 'completed']

    def list_clients(self, status: str = None) -> List[C2Client]:
        """List all clients."""
        if status:
            return [c for c in self.clients.values() if c.status == status]
        return list(self.clients.values())

    def list_channels(self) -> List[C2Channel]:
        """List all channels."""
        return list(self.channels.values())

    def list_tasks(self, status: str = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """List all tasks."""
        if status:
            return [t for t in self.tasks.values() if t['status'] == status]
        return list(self.tasks.values())

    def export_data(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        """Export C2 data."""
        data = {
            'version': '21.0',
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'clients': [c.to_dict() for c in self.clients.values()],
            'channels': [c.to_dict() for c in self.channels.values()],
            'tasks': list(self.tasks.values()),
        }
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[C2Server] Export failed: {e}")
            return False

    def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        """Get server statistics."""
        return dict(self.stats)
```

### 13. data_exfil.py

```python
"""
ExfilEngine - Data Exfiltration Engine
======================================
Advanced data exfiltration through multiple channels.
"""

import os
import re
import json
import time
import base64
import gzip
import struct
import hashlib
import threading
import subprocess
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Tuple, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto


class ExfilMethod(Enum):
    HTTP = auto()
    HTTPS = auto()
    DNS = auto()
    ICMP = auto()
    SMTP = auto()
    FTP = auto()
    SSH = auto()
    WEBSOCKET = auto()
    CLOUD = auto()
    BLUETOOTH = auto()
    WIFI = auto()
    RADIO = auto()
    SATELLITE = auto()
    STEGANOGRAPHY = auto()
    SOCIAL_MEDIA = auto()
    PASTEBIN = auto()
    GITHUB = auto()
    DISCORD = auto()
    TELEGRAM = auto()
    SLACK = auto()
    TOR = auto()
    I2P = auto()
    ENCRYPTED = auto()
    FRAGMENTED = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class ExfilChannel(Enum):
    DIRECT = auto()
    PROXY = auto()
    CDN = auto()
    MESH = auto()
    P2P = auto()
    DEAD_DROP = auto()
    TIMING = auto()
    STORAGE = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


@dataclass
class ExfilJob:
    """A data exfiltration job."""
    id: str
    source_path: str
    method: ExfilMethod
    channel: ExfilChannel
    destination: str
    size: int = 0
    compressed: bool = False
    encrypted: bool = False
    fragmented: bool = False
    status: str = "pending"
    progress: float = 0.0
    chunks_total: int = 0
    chunks_sent: int = 0
    start_time: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    end_time: Optional[float] = None
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'source_path': self.source_path,
            'method': self.method.name,
            'channel': self.channel.name,
            'destination': self.destination,
            'size': self.size,
            'compressed': self.compressed,
            'encrypted': self.encrypted,
            'fragmented': self.fragmented,
            'status': self.status,
            'progress': self.progress,
            'chunks_total': self.chunks_total,
            'chunks_sent': self.chunks_sent,
            'start_time': self.start_time,
            'end_time': self.end_time,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


class ExfilEngine:
    """Data exfiltration engine."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.jobs: Dict[str, ExfilJob] = {}
        self.stats = {
            'jobs_created': 0,
            'jobs_completed': 0,
            'jobs_failed': 0,
            'bytes_exfiltrated': 0,
            'chunks_sent': 0,
            'compression_ratio': 0.0,
        }

    def create_job(self, source_path: str, method: ExfilMethod,
                   channel: ExfilChannel, destination: str) -> ExfilJob:
        """Create an exfiltration job."""
        job_id = f"exf_{int(time.time())}_{len(self.jobs)}"
        size = os.path.getsize(source_path) if os.path.exists(source_path) else 0
        job = ExfilJob(
            id=job_id, source_path=source_path, method=method,
            channel=channel, destination=destination, size=size
        )
        self.jobs[job_id] = job
        self.stats['jobs_created'] += 1
        print(f"[ExfilEngine] Job created: {source_path} -> {destination} ({method.name})")
        return job

    def compress(self, job_id: str) -> bool:
        """Compress data before exfiltration."""
        job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
        if not job:
            return False
        print(f"[ExfilEngine] Compressing {job.source_path}")
        job.compressed = True
        job.size = int(job.size * 0.3)  # 70% compression
        return True

    def encrypt(self, job_id: str, key: bytes = None) -> bool:
        """Encrypt data before exfiltration."""
        job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
        if not job:
            return False
        print(f"[ExfilEngine] Encrypting {job.source_path}")
        job.encrypted = True
        return True

    def fragment(self, job_id: str, chunk_size: int = 1024) -> bool:
        """Fragment data into small chunks."""
        job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
        if not job:
            return False
        job.fragmented = True
        job.chunks_total = (job.size // chunk_size) + 1
        print(f"[ExfilEngine] Fragmenting into {job.chunks_total} chunks")
        return True

    def exfil_http(self, job_id: str, url: str, headers: Dict = None) -> bool:
        """Exfiltrate via HTTP POST."""
        job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
        if not job:
            return False
        print(f"[ExfilEngine] Exfiltrating via HTTP to {url}")
        job.status = "in_progress"
        job.chunks_sent = job.chunks_total
        job.progress = 100.0
        job.status = "completed"
        job.end_time = time.time()
        self.stats['jobs_completed'] += 1
        self.stats['bytes_exfiltrated'] += job.size
        return True

    def exfil_dns(self, job_id: str, domain: str) -> bool:
        """Exfiltrate via DNS queries."""
        job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
        if not job:
            return False
        print(f"[ExfilEngine] Exfiltrating via DNS to {domain}")
        job.status = "completed"
        job.end_time = time.time()
        self.stats['jobs_completed'] += 1
        self.stats['bytes_exfiltrated'] += job.size
        return True

    def exfil_icmp(self, job_id: str, target_ip: str) -> bool:
        """Exfiltrate via ICMP packets."""
        job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
        if not job:
            return False
        print(f"[ExfilEngine] Exfiltrating via ICMP to {target_ip}")
        job.status = "completed"
        job.end_time = time.time()
        self.stats['jobs_completed'] += 1
        self.stats['bytes_exfiltrated'] += job.size
        return True

    def exfil_steganography(self, job_id: str, carrier_path: str,
                            output_path: str) -> bool:
        """Exfiltrate via steganography."""
        job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
        if not job:
            return False
        print(f"[ExfilEngine] Exfiltrating via steganography: {carrier_path}")
        job.status = "completed"
        job.end_time = time.time()
        self.stats['jobs_completed'] += 1
        self.stats['bytes_exfiltrated'] += job.size
        return True

    def exfil_social(self, job_id: str, platform: str, account: str) -> bool:
        """Exfiltrate via social media."""
        job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
        if not job:
            return False
        print(f"[ExfilEngine] Exfiltrating via {platform} to {account}")
        job.status = "completed"
        job.end_time = time.time()
        self.stats['jobs_completed'] += 1
        self.stats['bytes_exfiltrated'] += job.size
        return True

    def exfil_cloud(self, job_id: str, provider: str, bucket: str) -> bool:
        """Exfiltrate to cloud storage."""
        job = self.jobs.get(job_id)
        if not job:
            return False
        print(f"[ExfilEngine] Exfiltrating to {provider}://{bucket}")
        job.status = "completed"
        job.end_time = time.time()
        self.stats['jobs_completed'] += 1
        self.stats['bytes_exfiltrated'] += job.size
        return True

    def auto_exfil(self, source_path: str, destination: str,
                   preferred_methods: List[ExfilMethod] = None) -> ExfilJob:
        """Automatically select best exfiltration method."""
        if preferred_methods is None:
            preferred_methods = [ExfilMethod.HTTPS, ExfilMethod.DNS, ExfilMethod.STEGANOGRAPHY]
        method = preferred_methods[0]
        job = self.create_job(source_path, method, ExfilChannel.DIRECT, destination)
        self.compress(job.id)
        self.encrypt(job.id)
        self.fragment(job.id)
        return job

    def list_jobs(self, status: str = None) -> List[ExfilJob]:
        """List all exfiltration jobs."""
        if status:
            return [j for j in self.jobs.values() if j.status == status]
        return list(self.jobs.values())

    def export_data(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        """Export exfiltration data."""
        data = {
            'version': '21.0',
            'timestamp': time.time(),
            'jobs': [j.to_dict() for j in self.jobs.values()],
        }
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ExfilEngine] Export failed: {e}")
            return False

    def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        """Get engine statistics."""
        return dict(self.stats)
```

"""
SocialEngineer - Social Engineering Toolkit
============================================
Phishing, pretexting, baiting, and other social engineering attacks.
"""

import os
import re
import json
import time
import base64
import hashlib
import random
import threading

    PHISHING = auto()
    SPEAR_PHISHING = auto()
    WHALING = auto()
    VISHING = auto()
    SMISHING = auto()
    BAITING = auto()
    PRETEXTING = auto()
    QUID_PRO_QUO = auto()
    TAILGATING = auto()
    SHOULDER_SURFING = auto()
    DUMPSTER_DIVING = auto()
    WATERING_HOLE = auto()
    SUPPLY_CHAIN = auto()
    IMPERSONATION = auto()
    REVERSE_SOCIAL = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class SETargetType(Enum):
    INDIVIDUAL = auto()
    EMPLOYEE = auto()
    EXECUTIVE = auto()
    IT_ADMIN = auto()
    HR = auto()
    FINANCE = auto()
    DEVELOPER = auto()
    CUSTOMER = auto()
    VENDOR = auto()
    PARTNER = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()

class SETarget:

    id: str
    name: str
    email: str = ""
    phone: str = ""
    title: str = ""
    department: str = ""
    company: str = ""
    target_type: SETargetType = SETargetType.UNKNOWN

    engagement_history: List[Dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    compromised: bool = False
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'name': self.name,
            'email': self.email,
            'phone': self.phone,
            'title': self.title,
            'department': self.department,
            'company': self.company,
            'target_type': self.target_type.name,
            'social_media': self.social_media,
            'interests': self.interests,
            'vulnerabilities': self.vulnerabilities,
            'engagement_history': self.engagement_history,
            'compromised': self.compromised,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


@dataclass
class SECampaign:
    """A social engineering campaign."""
    id: str
    name: str
    vector: SEVector
    targets: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    end_time: Optional[float] = None
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'name': self.name,
            'vector': self.vector.name,
            'targets': self.targets,
            'template': self.template,
            'status': self.status,
            'emails_sent': self.emails_sent,
            'emails_opened': self.emails_opened,
            'links_clicked': self.links_clicked,
            'credentials_captured': self.credentials_captured,
            'attachments_opened': self.attachments_opened,
            'start_time': self.start_time,
            'end_time': self.end_time,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


class SocialEngineer:
    """Social engineering toolkit."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.targets: Dict[str, SETarget] = {}
        self.campaigns: Dict[str, SECampaign] = {}
        self.templates: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.stats = {
            'targets_added': 0,
            'campaigns_created': 0,
            'emails_sent': 0,
            'emails_opened': 0,
            'links_clicked': 0,
            'credentials_captured': 0,
            'targets_compromised': 0,
        }

    def add_target(self, target_id: str, name: str, email: str = "",
                   title: str = "", company: str = "",
                   target_type: SETargetType = SETargetType.UNKNOWN) -> SETarget:
        """Add a social engineering target."""
        target = SETarget(
            id=target_id, name=name, email=email,
            title=title, company=company, target_type=target_type
        )
        self.targets[target_id] = target
        self.stats['targets_added'] += 1
        print(f"[SocialEngineer] Target added: {name} ({email})")
        return target

    def create_campaign(self, campaign_id: str, name: str,
                        vector: SEVector, template: str) -> SEC

## 1. `payload_builder.py` - Speichern unter `/mnt/agents/output/nsablocklist-v21/payload_builder.py`

```python
"""
PayloadBuilder - Payload Generation & Delivery
==============================================
Multi-platform payload generation and delivery system.
"""

import os
import re
import json
import time
import base64
import struct
import hashlib
import threading
import subprocess
from typing import Dict, List, Set, Optional, Tuple, Any, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto


class PayloadType(Enum):
    REVERSE_SHELL = auto()
    BIND_SHELL = auto()
    METERPRETER = auto()
    WEBSHELL = auto()
    STAGER = auto()
    DROPPER = auto()
    ROOTKIT = auto()
    KEYLOGGER = auto()
    RANSOMWARE = auto()
    TROJAN = auto()
    WORM = auto()
    BOT = auto()
    MINER = auto()
    SPYWARE = auto()
    ADWARE = auto()
    RAT = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class PayloadPlatform(Enum):
    WINDOWS = auto()
    LINUX = auto()
    MACOS = auto()
    ANDROID = auto()
    IOS = auto()
    BSD = auto()
    EMBEDDED = auto()
    WEB = auto()
    CROSS_PLATFORM = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class PayloadArch(Enum):
    X86 = auto()
    X64 = auto()
    ARM = auto()
    ARM64 = auto()
    MIPS = auto()
    MIPS64 = auto()
    PPC = auto()
    SPARC = auto()
    WASM = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


class PayloadDelivery(Enum):
    DIRECT = auto()
    EMAIL = auto()
    WEB = auto()
    USB = auto()
    NETWORK = auto()
    SOCIAL = auto()
    SUPPLY_CHAIN = auto()
    WATERING_HOLE = auto()
    BROWSER = auto()
    APP_STORE = auto()
    UNKNOWN = auto()


@dataclass
class Payload:
    """A generated payload."""
    id: str
    name: str
    payload_type: PayloadType
    platform: PayloadPlatform
    architecture: PayloadArch
    delivery: PayloadDelivery
    size: int = 0
    encoded: bool = False
    encrypted: bool = False
    obfuscated: bool = False
    shellcode: bytes = b""
    source_code: str = ""
    compiled: bool = False
    staged: bool = False
    stage_size: int = 0
    callbacks: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    features: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id, 'name': self.name,
            'payload_type': self.payload_type.name,
            'platform': self.platform.name,
            'architecture': self.architecture.name,
            'delivery': self.delivery.name,
            'size': self.size, 'encoded': self.encoded,
            'encrypted': self.encrypted, 'obfuscated': self.obfuscated,
            'shellcode_b64': base64.b64encode(self.shellcode).decode() if self.shellcode else "",
            'compiled': self.compiled, 'staged': self.staged,
            'stage_size': self.stage_size,
            'callbacks': self.callbacks, 'features': self.features,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


@dataclass
class PayloadDelivery:
    """A payload delivery mechanism."""
    id: str
    payload_id: str
    method: PayloadDelivery
    target: str = ""
    status: str = "pending"
    delivered: bool = False
    executed: bool = False
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            'id': self.id, 'payload_id': self.payload_id,
            'method': self.method.name, 'target': self.target,
            'status': self.status, 'delivered': self.delivered,
            'executed': self.executed, 'timestamp': self.timestamp,
            'metadata': self.metadata,
        }


class PayloadBuilder:
    """Multi-platform payload builder."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.payloads: Dict[str, Payload] = {}
        self.deliveries: Dict[str, PayloadDelivery] = {}
        self.stats = {
            'payloads_generated': 0, 'payloads_encoded': 0,
            'payloads_encrypted': 0, 'payloads_delivered': 0,
            'payloads_executed': 0, 'delivery_attempts': 0,
        }

    def generate(self, payload_type: PayloadType, platform: PayloadPlatform,
                 architecture: PayloadArch, delivery: PayloadDelivery,
                 callbacks: List[str] = None, features: List[str] = None) -> Payload:
        payload_id = f"pld_{int(time.time())}_{len(self.payloads)}"
        shellcode = b"\x90" * 512
        payload = Payload(
            id=payload_id, name=f"{payload_type.name}_{platform.name}",
            payload_type=payload_type, platform=platform,
            architecture=architecture, delivery=delivery,
            size=len(shellcode), shellcode=shellcode,
            callbacks=callbacks or [], features=features or []
        )
        self.payloads[payload_id] = payload
        self.stats['payloads_generated'] += 1
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Generated {payload_type.name} for {platform.name}/{architecture.name}")
        return payload

    def encode(self, payload_id: str, encoder: str = "x64/xor") -> bool:
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload: return False
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Encoding {payload_id} with {encoder}")
        payload.encoded = True
        self.stats['payloads_encoded'] += 1
        return True

    def encrypt(self, payload_id: str, key: str = "") -> bool:
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload: return False
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Encrypting {payload_id}")
        payload.encrypted = True
        self.stats['payloads_encrypted'] += 1
        return True

    def obfuscate(self, payload_id: str) -> bool:
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload: return False
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Obfuscating {payload_id}")
        payload.obfuscated = True
        return True

    def compile_payload(self, payload_id: str, compiler: str = "gcc") -> bool:
        payload = self.payloads.get(payload_id)
        if not payload: return False
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Compiling {payload_id} with {compiler}")
        payload.compiled = True
        return True

    def deliver(self, payload_id: str, target: str, method: PayloadDelivery) -> PayloadDelivery:
        delivery_id = f"dlv_{int(time.time())}"
        delivery = PayloadDelivery(
            id=delivery_id, payload_id=payload_id,
            method=method, target=target
        )
        self.deliveries[delivery_id] = delivery
        self.stats['delivery_attempts'] += 1
        print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Delivering {payload_id} to {target} via {method.name}")
        return delivery

    def mark_delivered(self, delivery_id: str) -> bool:
        delivery = self.deliveries.get(delivery_id)
        if not delivery: return False
        delivery.delivered = True
        delivery.status = "delivered"
        self.stats['payloads_delivered'] += 1
        return True

    def mark_executed(self, delivery_id: str) -> bool:
        delivery = self.deliveries.get(delivery_id)
        if not delivery: return False
        delivery.executed = True
        delivery.status = "executed"
        self.stats['payloads_executed'] += 1
        return True

    def generate_stager(self, platform: PayloadPlatform, callback_url: str,
                        stage_size: int = 4096) -> Payload:
        payload = self.generate(
            PayloadType.STAGER, platform, PayloadArch.X64,
            PayloadDelivery.DIRECT, callbacks=[callback_url]
        )
        payload.staged = True
        payload.stage_size = stage_size
        return payload

    def generate_reverse_shell(self, platform: PayloadPlatform, lhost: str,
                              lport: int, arch: PayloadArch = PayloadArch.X64) -> Payload:
        return self.generate(
            PayloadType.REVERSE_SHELL, platform, arch,
            PayloadDelivery.DIRECT, callbacks=[f"{lhost}:{lport}"],
            features=["TCP", "encrypted", "persistent"]
        )

    def generate_webshell(self, language: str = "php", password: str = "jayjay") -> Payload:
        return self.generate(
            PayloadType.WEBSHELL, PayloadPlatform.WEB, PayloadArch.WASM,
            PayloadDelivery.WEB, features=[language, f"password={password}"]
        )

    def list_payloads(self) -> List[Payload]:
        return list(self.payloads.values())

    def list_deliveries(self) -> List[PayloadDelivery]:
        return list(self.deliveries.values())

    def export_data(self, filepath: str) -> bool:
        data = {
            'version': '21.0', 'timestamp': time.time(),
            'payloads': [p.to_dict() for p in self.payloads.values()],
            'deliveries': [d.to_dict() for d in self.deliveries.values()],
        }
        try:
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[PayloadBuilder] Export failed: {e}")
            return False

    def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        return dict(self.stats)
```
